Bounded PE/objdump supplements, checked against the same executable:
Vtable +D4: 0109B5AC -> A5DBE0; 0109CD44 and 0109D454 -> A7FD90; 010ADCF8 -> B5F9B0; 010C1D14 -> C46F60.
Basis globals at 011A946C..011A948F contain f32 (1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1) in the file. This is not a proof of runtime immutability.
BB4F40 owned entry bytes: 89 44 24 24 89 6C 24 28; stores EAX to S+24 and EBP to S+28.
BB4F7A bytes: 8D 44 24 1C B3 01 50; cursor address S+1C, BL=1, PUSH cursor.
C38866 bytes: C7 45 94 00 00 00 00; native plane index [EBP-6C]=0, seven-byte instruction.
BB48A2 pushes EBX; on the cached refresh route S=entry ESP-5C after locals 4C and saved EBP/ESI/EDI/EBX.
BB4F83 writes BL at ESP+68 after PUSH, hence S+64. BB4FA2 writes count directly at S+64. BB4FB6 reads S+64 for growth.
BB4FD4 reads slot zero from S+24. BB5003 restores outer EBP from S+28. BB5097 publishes next outer index to S+28.
BB50AD restores geometry EBP from S+60. BB50B5 reloads key from S+20. These correct radare2's overlapping inferred variable names.
Sorter frame T: after aligned local allocation and EBX/ESI/EDI pushes, T+13 is changed byte, T+14 property, T+18 original next node, T+1C current f32 key, T+20 f64 comparison temporary; EBP+8 bound argument.
Relative-branch byte discovery in BB4740..BB5C86 found candidate BB4F88 -> BB4F40. BB4F88 is inside the displacement of CALL at BB4F87, so it is rejected, not an incoming branch. The AABB owned-window byte discovery found none. These bounded scans are not computed-edge or whole-binary coverage.

Capture limitations and rejected discovery windows:
pass_before_window BB4ED0, pass_after_window BB5BE0, render_callback_registration B57690, compound_builder_end B5F90E, node_visible A5DC00, compound_free C47740, compound_current_scene_swap B5FB42 start inside instructions. Their leading bytes are rejected. Corrected aligned owning entries/ranges are captured separately; subsequent independently aligned instructions can be referenced only through those corrected captures.
geometry_visible A80710 is the proven UpdateWorldBound method, not OnVisible. The correct geometry OnVisible target A7FD90 was verified by a bounded PE vtable read and is captured separately.
refresh_full and compound_scene_builder_full results were cut at the service output limit, mid-instruction; they are partial, not complete function listings. accumulator_init_full, scene_visibility, compound_culler_outer and node_visibility_complete end in their actual RET and provide complete respective bodies.
Empty xref results (including unqualified discovery addresses C4EC00 and C4F3A0) do not prove absence of callers. No contract is assigned to those exploratory addresses.


## Worker pre-phase receiver and scope-helper follow-up

These captures refine 0x0086FD70's three pre-phase callees. Labels containing lock were exploratory; 0x00404EB0/0x00404F00/0x00404F50/0x00404F30 prove TLS state capture/publication, not a lock. Wrapper/getter/scope bodies below end at RET and are complete. prephase_target1, prephase_target2 and prephase_target3 are bounded partial function prefixes (260-line request); no complete callback, wait, writer or purity conclusion is assigned to those larger bodies. Embedded InterfaceManager.cpp text is executable evidence of their native subsystem.
